.char{font-size:28px;font-weight:700;color:#2C1810}.letter-item.correct{background:#d4edda!important;border-color:#28a745!important;border-width:4px!important}.letter-item.wrong{background:#f8d7da!important;border-color:#dc3545!important;border-width:4px!important}.feedback{position:absolute;top:10px;right:10px;font-size:30px;font-weight:700;display:none}.letter-item.correct 0.2s;min-height:220px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;text-align:center;overflow:hidden}.word-card:hover{transform:translateY(-5px)}.word-card .emoji{font-size:120px;margin-bottom:15px}.word-card .word{font-size:32px;font-weight:700;margin:10px 0}.word-card .word .wh-red{color:#C8102E}.word-card .word .black{color:#2C1810}.word-card .translation{font-size:20px;color:#666;margin-top:10px;filter:blur(8px);transition:filter 0.4s ease;user-select:none}.word-card.revealed .translation{filter:blur(0)}.word-card .check{position:absolute;top:12px;right:12px;width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:50%;background:#FFD700;color:#C8102E;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:20px;opacity:0;transition:opacity
